# Commands · P · part 1

1,456 entries, p to pentest. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **p**: Shortcut for "parallelize" — take the work in flight (or the named work), split off the parts that do not depend on each other, and run them at the same time so it finishes sooner. "p" splits what clearly splits, "pp" splits harder, "ppp" is maximum parallelism; "p1", "p2", "p3" are the same three levels. "ff" and "fff" are levels 2 and 3 too; a single "f" is the fast-mode toggle, not a level. Bare forms mean the same thing. ([danielmiessler/LifeOS](https://github.com/danielmiessler/LifeOS/blob/HEAD/LifeOS/install/commands/p.md) · ★19k)
- **p**: Prompt optimizer — rewrite this request into an optimized prompt (ultrathink), then carry it out ([davepoon/buildwithclaude](https://github.com/davepoon/buildwithclaude/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude-prompt/commands/p.md) · ★3.5k)
- **p**: Alias for /parallel — parallelize independent work to its real resource ceiling ([jleechanorg/jleechan-skills](https://github.com/jleechanorg/jleechan-skills/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/p.md) · ★4)
- **p**: $ARGUMENTS ([bearingfruitco/cc-boiler-js-public](https://github.com/bearingfruitco/cc-boiler-js-public/blob/HEAD/.claude-backup-before-cleanup-20250131/commands/p.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **p**: Prompt optimizer · clarify only if needed, rewrite your request into a sharper prompt, then carry it out. Prefix --dry to stop at the rewrite. ([danielkremen818/claude-prompt](https://github.com/danielkremen818/claude-prompt/blob/HEAD/commands/p.md) · ★2)
- **p**: Plan a new work item (Epic/Story/Task/Bug) ([FlojoInc/balustrade](https://github.com/FlojoInc/balustrade/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/p.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **p**: Shortcut for /principles ([AdeptMind/pr-emojis-in-slack](https://github.com/AdeptMind/pr-emojis-in-slack/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/p.md) · 4 copies)
- **p**: **Shortcut:** `/p` (Production - This Chat Only) ([gotekna/XTeeem-OLD](https://github.com/gotekna/XTeeem-OLD/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/p.md))
- **p**: Run browser-based performance checks AND backend performance analysis. **Automatically fix any slowness found.** ([gotekna/XTeeem-OLD](https://github.com/gotekna/XTeeem-OLD/blob/HEAD/backend/.claude/commands/p.md))
- **p0**: P0 变更检测 ([pwl1987/flow-kit](https://github.com/pwl1987/flow-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/p0.md) · ★1)
- **p0-approval**: flow-kit 命令: p0-approval ([pwl1987/flow-kit](https://github.com/pwl1987/flow-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/p0-approval.md) · ★1)
- **p0-approval**: &gt; 【CLAUDE CODE INSTRUCTION 强制约束】 &gt; 本文件实现 P0 审批工作流。 &gt; D4-8: P0 Approval Workflow ([pwl1987/flow-kit](https://github.com/pwl1987/flow-kit/blob/HEAD/flow-kit/commands/p0-approval.md) · ★1)
- **p0-market**: Delivers a first market assessment: Is there demand? Who are the competitors? Is the idea roughly viable economically? The result is an overview needed for the Go/No-Go Decision at Gate 0. ([jonase47/ccpr](https://github.com/jonase47/ccpr/blob/HEAD/commands/p0-market.md) · ★1)
- **p0-problem**: Defines the core problem of a project or business idea and identifies the first rough Target Audience. The result is a clear problem statement that serves as the foundation for all subsequent phases. ([jonase47/ccpr](https://github.com/jonase47/ccpr/blob/HEAD/commands/p0-problem.md) · ★1)
- **p0-regulatory**: Identifies regulatory requirements, DSGVO (GDPR) obligations, and legal knock-out criteria early on, before significant time is invested in the idea. A regulatory showstopper should be recognised as early as possible. ([jonase47/ccpr](https://github.com/jonase47/ccpr/blob/HEAD/commands/p0-regulatory.md) · ★1)
- **p1**: Parallelize at level 1 — split the parts that clearly split. The same as "p"; the full rule is /p. Bare "p1" means the same thing. ([danielmiessler/LifeOS](https://github.com/danielmiessler/LifeOS/blob/HEAD/LifeOS/install/commands/p1.md) · ★19k)
- **p1-business-model**: Develops the Business Model with Business Model Canvas, sharpens the value proposition, and defines a first pricing approach. The result is BUSINESS_MODEL.md as the economic foundation of the project. ([jonase47/ccpr](https://github.com/jonase47/ccpr/blob/HEAD/commands/p1-business-model.md) · ★1)
- **p1-features**: Defines the feature scope of the project, prioritises using MoSCoW, and clearly defines the MVP scope. The result is FEATURES.md and MVP.md as the binding basis for architecture and planning. ([jonase47/ccpr](https://github.com/jonase47/ccpr/blob/HEAD/commands/p1-features.md) · ★1)
- **p1-financial-plan**: Creates a first financial plan with Cost Structure, Revenue Forecast, and break-even analysis. The goal is not exact bookkeeping but a plausible set of numbers that demonstrates the economic viability of the venture. ([jonase47/ccpr](https://github.com/jonase47/ccpr/blob/HEAD/commands/p1-financial-plan.md) · ★1)
- **p1-journeys**: Develops detailed personas, user journeys, and usage scenarios based on the Discovery results. These artefacts form the user foundation for all further conception decisions. ([jonase47/ccpr](https://github.com/jonase47/ccpr/blob/HEAD/commands/p1-journeys.md) · ★1)
- **p1-privacy**: Conducts a systematic initial assessment of data protection requirements: What personal data is processed, on what legal basis, and what DSGVO (GDPR) obligations arise from this? The result is a well-founded DSGVO initial assessment as a mandatory component of the Concept. ([jonase47/ccpr](https://github.com/jonase47/ccpr/blob/HEAD/commands/p1-privacy.md) · ★1)
- **p10**: PUA P10 CTO 模式 — 定战略管 P9。/pua:p10。Triggers on: '/pua:p10', 'pua p10', 'CTO模式', 'P10', '战略规划', '架构委员会'. ([tanweai/pua](https://github.com/tanweai/pua/blob/HEAD/commands/p10.md) · ★20k · 2 copies)
- **p2**: Parallelize at level 2 — split harder, with checks running beside the build. The same as "pp" and "ff"; the full rule is /p. Bare "p2" means the same thing. ([danielmiessler/LifeOS](https://github.com/danielmiessler/LifeOS/blob/HEAD/LifeOS/install/commands/p2.md) · ★19k)
- **p2-assumptions**: Systematically lists all critical Assumptions from the conception phase and prioritises them by risk and validatability. The result is an Assumptions register that serves as the working basis for all further validation steps. ([jonase47/ccpr](https://github.com/jonase47/ccpr/blob/HEAD/commands/p2-assumptions.md) · ★1)
- **p2-commit-push-pr**: コミット、プッシュ、ブランチからPR作成（resolve自動指定） ([p2hacks2025/pre-03](https://github.com/p2hacks2025/pre-03/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/p2-commit-push-pr.md) · ★4)
- **p2-market-validation**: Checks critical market, pricing, and demand Assumptions from the Assumptions register using concrete research, data, and competitive analyses. The result is confirmed or refuted Assumptions as the basis for informed Concept decisions. ([jonase47/ccpr](https://github.com/jonase47/ccpr/blob/HEAD/commands/p2-market-validation.md) · ★1)
- **p2-poc**: Builds a minimal technical Proof of Concept for the riskiest technical area of the project. The goal is not a finished feature but proof that the planned approach fundamentally works – before investing in architecture and implementation. ([jonase47/ccpr](https://github.com/jonase47/ccpr/blob/HEAD/commands/p2-poc.md) · ★1)
- **p2-regulatory-check**: Validates the regulatory Assumptions from Phase 1 concretely and practically: Is the venture legally implementable? Which hurdles are real, which are surmountable? The result is a robust regulatory validation as the Decision basis for Gate 2. ([jonase47/ccpr](https://github.com/jonase47/ccpr/blob/HEAD/commands/p2-regulatory-check.md) · ★1)
- **p2-review**: PRをレビューしGitHubにコメント投稿 ([p2hacks2025/pre-03](https://github.com/p2hacks2025/pre-03/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/p2-review.md) · ★4)

- **p3**: Parallelize at level 3 — maximum parallelism, fanned out as a Workflow. The same as "ppp" and "fff"; the full rule is /p. Bare "p3" means the same thing. ([danielmiessler/LifeOS](https://github.com/danielmiessler/LifeOS/blob/HEAD/LifeOS/install/commands/p3.md) · ★19k)

- **p3-sec-data**: Defines encryption (transit/rest), secrets management and backup security. ([jonase47/ccpr](https://github.com/jonase47/ccpr/blob/HEAD/commands/p3-sec-data.md) · ★1)

- **p4-setup**: Sets up the repository, CI/CD pipeline and local development environment. The result is a working development infrastructure in which the team can immediately start with `/p5-implement`. ([jonase47/ccpr](https://github.com/jonase47/ccpr/blob/HEAD/commands/p4-setup.md) · ★1)
- **p4-sprint**: Plans the current sprint: pulls matching stories from the backlog, defines the sprint goal, identifies risks, and creates the sprint document. Called repeatedly at the start of each new sprint. ([jonase47/ccpr](https://github.com/jonase47/ccpr/blob/HEAD/commands/p4-sprint.md) · ★1)
- **p5-acceptance**: Tests an implemented feature against its requirements from a user perspective: are all acceptance criteria met? Are edge cases handled correctly? The result is test findings that determine whether the feature counts as "Done". ([jonase47/ccpr](https://github.com/jonase47/ccpr/blob/HEAD/commands/p5-acceptance.md) · ★1)
- **p5-bugfix**: Systematically analyzes a found bug, identifies the root cause and fixes it with an accompanying regression test. Goal: the bug is fixed, secured by a test, and cannot be silently reintroduced. ([jonase47/ccpr](https://github.com/jonase47/ccpr/blob/HEAD/commands/p5-bugfix.md) · ★1)
- **p5-docs**: Keeps code documentation in sync with the implemented code: inline comments, API documentation, changes to README or CONTRIBUTING, and updates to technical specifications if the code deviates from them. Called after implementing a feature. ([jonase47/ccpr](https://github.com/jonase47/ccpr/blob/HEAD/commands/p5-docs.md) · ★1)
- **p5-impl-green**: Writes the minimal production code that makes the existing tests green. No over-engineering. ([jonase47/ccpr](https://github.com/jonase47/ccpr/blob/HEAD/commands/p5-impl-green.md) · ★1)
- **p5-impl-red**: Writes unit tests for a feature BEFORE production code exists. Tests must fail. ([jonase47/ccpr](https://github.com/jonase47/ccpr/blob/HEAD/commands/p5-impl-red.md) · ★1)
- **p5-impl-refactor**: Improves code quality without changing behavior. All tests must continue to pass. ([jonase47/ccpr](https://github.com/jonase47/ccpr/blob/HEAD/commands/p5-impl-refactor.md) · ★1)
- **p5-implement**: Implements a feature in the TDD cycle: Red → Green → Refactor. Each phase is its own sub-command with a focused agent call. ([jonase47/ccpr](https://github.com/jonase47/ccpr/blob/HEAD/commands/p5-implement.md) · ★1)
- **p5-polish**: Collects small clean-up TODOs accumulated during the sprint (from `/p5-review`, `/p5-acceptance`, `/p5-bugfix`, TODO/FIXME comments) that do not justify a full story but make the next sprint easier. Triages each item into `polish-now`, `backlog`, `handover`, or `drop`. Optionally executes `polish-now` items directly (TDD mini-cycle, one commit per item). Runs **between `/gate-p5` (Sprint Done) and ([jonase47/ccpr](https://github.com/jonase47/ccpr/blob/HEAD/commands/p5-polish.md) · ★1)
- **p5-review**: Conducts a structured code review: first code quality, then security. Each dimension is its own sub-command. ([jonase47/ccpr](https://github.com/jonase47/ccpr/blob/HEAD/commands/p5-review.md) · ★1)
- **p5-review-code**: Checks code for quality, clean code, logic errors and test coverage. Delivers review findings with severity level. ([jonase47/ccpr](https://github.com/jonase47/ccpr/blob/HEAD/commands/p5-review-code.md) · ★1)
- **p5-review-security**: Checks code against the security checklist. Focus on OWASP Top 10, injection, auth and data handling. ([jonase47/ccpr](https://github.com/jonase47/ccpr/blob/HEAD/commands/p5-review-security.md) · ★1)
- **p5-review-sprint**: Runs ONE final code review over the **entire sprint** at sprint end, with a stronger model, to catch issues that single-story reviews structurally cannot see: cross-story interactions, the schema/data model as a whole, conformance to ADRs and Constitution Inviolables, and consistency with prior lessons/instincts. - **p6-a11y-keyboard**: Checks full keyboard operability: tab order, focus indicator, Enter/Space, Escape, focus trap. ([jonase47/ccpr](https://github.com/jonase47/ccpr/blob/HEAD/commands/p6-a11y-keyboard.md) · ★1)

- **p6-exploratory**: Conducts unstructured, experience-based tests that automated tests and defined test cases do not cover. - **p6-functional**: Runs systematic functional tests at the system level. Each test level is a separate sub-command. ([jonase47/ccpr](https://github.com/jonase47/ccpr/blob/HEAD/commands/p6-functional.md) · ★1)
- **p6-pentest**: Conducts an active attack simulation. Each attack phase is a separate sub-command. ([jonase47/ccpr](https://github.com/jonase47/ccpr/blob/HEAD/commands/p6-pentest.md) · ★1)

- **paper-trail-acquire**: Troisième passe du pipeline cible refondu. Lance la cascade PDF (10 sources : Crossref OA + arXiv + OpenAlex + Unpaywall + HAL + CORE + éditeur par DOI + archive.org + WebSearch ; jusqu'à 17 avec les voies par navigateur et les sources étendues opt-in) ciblée sur les refs d'un SOTA donné. Différent de `pipeline run` qui itère sur TOUTES les refs actives — `acquire` ne traite que les refs citées par le SOTA en cours. ([roomi-fields/paper-trail](https://github.com/roomi-fields/paper-trail/blob/HEAD/commands/paper-trail-acquire.md) · ★5)
- **paper-trail-audit-all**: Batch audit of every SOTA in the vault. For each SOTA, identifies refs that are page1_validated (technical OK) but not yet sota_cited_confirmed (content audited), and launches audit-sota on those SOTAs. Returns a per-SOTA summary at the end. ([roomi-fields/paper-trail](https://github.com/roomi-fields/paper-trail/blob/HEAD/commands/paper-trail-audit-all.md) · ★5)
- **paper-trail-audit-article**: Per-citation audit of an academic article (LaTeX or Markdown). Produces RECEIPTS.md classifying each citation as VALID/ADJUST/INVALID/UNVERIFIABLE. Optional --warn inserts inline warnings in a .bak copy of the source. ([roomi-fields/paper-trail](https://github.com/roomi-fields/paper-trail/blob/HEAD/commands/paper-trail-audit-article.md) · ★5)
- **paper-trail-audit-sota**: Audit a SOTA's bibliography. Classify each cited ref by validation state. Optional --purge removes citations to retracted refs from the SOTA (with .bak backup). ([roomi-fields/paper-trail](https://github.com/roomi-fields/paper-trail/blob/HEAD/commands/paper-trail-audit-sota.md) · ★5)
- **paper-trail-cascade**: Acquire PDFs via the 8-source cascade (11 with opt-in extended sources) for a single ref by slug, or a batch filtered by state. Validates page 1 anti-homonymy on each download. ([roomi-fields/paper-trail](https://github.com/roomi-fields/paper-trail/blob/HEAD/commands/paper-trail-cascade.md) · ★5)
- **paper-trail-decide**: Inspect a single reference in detail (identity, PDF status, acquisition history with verdicts, citation context in vault, state transitions) and decide its fate. Offers actionable decisions based on the ref's current state. ([roomi-fields/paper-trail](https://github.com/roomi-fields/paper-trail/blob/HEAD/commands/paper-trail-decide.md) · ★5)
- **paper-trail-doctor**: Audit the registry via the 19 mechanical invariants (I1-I19). Reports ERROR/WARN/INFO violations. Optional auto-fix for safe ones (I4 path, I6 sha256, I9 attempts). ([roomi-fields/paper-trail](https://github.com/roomi-fields/paper-trail/blob/HEAD/commands/paper-trail-doctor.md) · ★5)
- **paper-trail-identify**: Première passe du pipeline cible refondu (read-only). Produit un rapport d'identification : pour chaque mention citée dans le SOTA, indique si la fiche existe déjà au registre, son état actuel, si elle a un PDF, et l'action recommandée (réutiliser / créer / passer en revue). Ne modifie ni le SOTA ni le registre. ([roomi-fields/paper-trail](https://github.com/roomi-fields/paper-trail/blob/HEAD/commands/paper-trail-identify.md) · ★5)
- **paper-trail-ingest**: Ingest the citations of one SOTA into the registry. Extracts bibliographic sections, parses citations via the citation-parser sub-agent, resolves DOI via Crossref/S2, deduplicates against the registry, creates new candidate refs, and substitutes free-text citations with [[wikilinks]]. Required first step to make a legacy SOTA conform to the paper-trail pipeline. ([roomi-fields/paper-trail](https://github.com/roomi-fields/paper-trail/blob/HEAD/commands/paper-trail-ingest.md) · ★5)
- **paper-trail-ingest-all**: Batch INGEST across the entire vault. Scans all SOTAs, identifies those with bibliographic sections containing free-text citations, then runs /paper-trail:ingest on each one sequentially. Reports per-SOTA summary at the end. The single batch entry point to make a legacy vault fully conform to the paper-trail pipeline. ([roomi-fields/paper-trail](https://github.com/roomi-fields/paper-trail/blob/HEAD/commands/paper-trail-ingest-all.md) · ★5)
- **paper-trail-inject-url**: Injecte une URL OA connue (HAL, dépôt uni, NIME, page perso) pour une ref dont la cascade automatique a échoué, puis relance l'acquisition + validation page 1. ([roomi-fields/paper-trail](https://github.com/roomi-fields/paper-trail/blob/HEAD/commands/paper-trail-inject-url.md) · ★5)
- **paper-trail-linkify**: Dernière passe du pipeline cible refondu. Insère les wikilinks finaux dans le SOTA (vers PDF si validé, sinon vers ancre dans une section `## Statut des sources` régénérée idempotemment en bas du fichier). Présuppose que les passes identify/purge/acquire ont été lancées. ([roomi-fields/paper-trail](https://github.com/roomi-fields/paper-trail/blob/HEAD/commands/paper-trail-linkify.md) · ★5)
- **paper-trail-new-paper**: Start writing an academic paper (IMRaD structure) on a topic, with anti-hallucination citation verification at every step. Builds on existing audited SOTAs. ([roomi-fields/paper-trail](https://github.com/roomi-fields/paper-trail/blob/HEAD/commands/paper-trail-new-paper.md) · ★5)
- **paper-trail-new-sota**: Create a new State-of-the-Art review on a topic, guaranteed without hallucinated citations. Inverted workflow (research → acquire PDFs → read → write), refuses to write from memory. ([roomi-fields/paper-trail](https://github.com/roomi-fields/paper-trail/blob/HEAD/commands/paper-trail-new-sota.md) · ★5)
- **paper-trail-purge**: Nettoie les wikilinks invalides d'un SOTA (vers refs retracted, refs `_0000_*` orphelines, suffixes numériques `_2_3_4`, fichiers techniques `20_ATLAS/`, `30_DEV/`, `.canvas`, slugs non-bibliographiques). Opère uniquement sur le SOTA, jamais sur le registre. Backup git auto avant `--apply`. ([roomi-fields/paper-trail](https://github.com/roomi-fields/paper-trail/blob/HEAD/commands/paper-trail-purge.md) · ★5)
- **paper-trail-reactivate-ocr**: Re-evaluate refs in state `awaiting_rtfm_ocr` via `rtfm check`. Transitions to `page1_validated` if OCR succeeded, or `needs_reacquisition` if OCR failed. ([roomi-fields/paper-trail](https://github.com/roomi-fields/paper-trail/blob/HEAD/commands/paper-trail-reactivate-ocr.md) · ★5)
- **paper-trail-receipts**: Local-only per-citation audit (no remote API). Reads the cited PDFs from the local registry, generates RECEIPTS.md. Faster than audit-article (no Crossref / paper-search calls). ([roomi-fields/paper-trail](https://github.com/roomi-fields/paper-trail/blob/HEAD/commands/paper-trail-receipts.md) · ★5)
- **paper-trail-registry-cleanup**: Nettoyage historique du registre des fiches bibliographiques (les ~900 fiches existantes). Cible les vieilles fiches mal nommées (`*_0000_*`, `_untitled`) ET les duplicates avec suffixes numériques (`foo_2020_bar_2`, `_2_3_4`) qui sont des artefacts de runs INGEST passés. Délègue les décisions au sub-agent `textbook-resolver`. Les merge sont propagés automatiquement vers tous les SOTAs via `sota_sync`. ([roomi-fields/paper-trail](https://github.com/roomi-fields/paper-trail/blob/HEAD/commands/paper-trail-registry-cleanup.md) · ★5)
- **paper-trail-resolve-textbooks**: Resolve incomplete textbook references in the registry. Reads candidates via `pipeline resolve-textbooks --list`, invokes the `textbook-resolver` sub-agent to produce a decisions JSON (merge_into / complete / blocked), and applies via `pipeline resolve-textbooks --apply-from`. Fully automated cleanup pass after INGEST. ([roomi-fields/paper-trail](https://github.com/roomi-fields/paper-trail/blob/HEAD/commands/paper-trail-resolve-textbooks.md) · ★5)
- **paper-trail-review**: Review and arbitrate problematic references (cascade-exhausted, unresolved UID, awaiting OCR). Generates an up-to-date report, walks through cases by category, and applies arbitration decisions to the registry. ([roomi-fields/paper-trail](https://github.com/roomi-fields/paper-trail/blob/HEAD/commands/paper-trail-review.md) · ★5)
- **paper-trail-search**: Search the validated registry for references matching a topic (author, title, year, slug). Returns only refs in state page1_validated or sota_cited_confirmed by default — i.e. references that have been technically validated (PDF acquired, page 1 OK) or fully audited (content validated against PDF). Used by sota-writer / paper-writer to find truthful sources, and by the user to query what's been confirmed. ([roomi-fields/paper-trail](https://github.com/roomi-fields/paper-trail/blob/HEAD/commands/paper-trail-search.md) · ★5)
- **paper-trail-status**: Show the registry status — count of refs per FSM state. Read-only, fast (&lt; 5s). ([roomi-fields/paper-trail](https://github.com/roomi-fields/paper-trail/blob/HEAD/commands/paper-trail-status.md) · ★5)
- **paper-verify**: Run the final verification gate (skills/verify-gate) and write out/report.md. ([AnasSAV/AI-research-paper-writer](https://github.com/AnasSAV/AI-research-paper-writer/blob/HEAD/commands/paper-verify.md))
- **paper-verify-experiments**: Verify experimental claims in ML papers against source code repositories. Use when asked to "verify experiments", "check claims against code", "fact-check results", "audit experiments", or "validate paper against repo". Cross-references paper statements with actual implementation. ([rpatrik96/research-agora](https://github.com/rpatrik96/research-agora/blob/HEAD/plugins/verify/commands/paper-verify-experiments.md) · ★17)
- **paper-vision**: Vision-model critic for the paper skill. Renders the paper source to PDF and per-page PNGs, then uses a vision-language model to score rendered-only defects (figure legibility, table overflow, palette adherence, mathtext artifacts) that the prose/citation critics never see. ([rjwalters/anvil](https://github.com/rjwalters/anvil/blob/HEAD/anvil/skills/paper/commands/paper-vision.md) · ★1)

- **paper-write**: Run the PaperForge pipeline from intake through drafting, stopping at the human gates. ([AnasSAV/AI-research-paper-writer](https://github.com/AnasSAV/AI-research-paper-writer/blob/HEAD/commands/paper-write.md))

- **paperfit**: **作用**: `/paperfit` 是 Claude Code 中的 PaperFit 主入口。用户可以直接用自然语言描述任务，例如排版分析、完整修复、模板迁移、长度调整、局部表格修复、视觉检查或状态查询。Agent 负责解析意图、识别主 `.tex`、判断是否需要画像、并自动进入对应的 PaperFit 子流程。 ([OpenRaiser/PaperFit](https://github.com/OpenRaiser/PaperFit/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/paperfit.md) · ★336)
- **paperfit**: PaperFit 的统一自然语言入口。 ([OpenRaiser/PaperFit](https://github.com/OpenRaiser/PaperFit/blob/HEAD/plugins/paperfit/commands/paperfit.md) · ★336)
- **paperfit-priority**: 查看或调整当前修复优先级。 ([OpenRaiser/PaperFit](https://github.com/OpenRaiser/PaperFit/blob/HEAD/plugins/paperfit/commands/paperfit-priority.md) · ★336)
- **paperfit-priority**: **作用**: 读取当前状态、视觉重点和修复计划，给出本轮应该先处理什么；若用户明确指定优先顺序，可将该顺序写入 `next_actions` 供后续 `/fix-layout` 直接遵循。 ([OpenRaiser/PaperFit](https://github.com/OpenRaiser/PaperFit/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/paperfit-priority.md) · ★336)
- **paperfit-undo**: **作用**: 恢复最近一次 PaperFit 自动写回前的备份版本，优先回滚主 `.tex`，必要时一并恢复 `data/state.json`。 ([OpenRaiser/PaperFit](https://github.com/OpenRaiser/PaperFit/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/paperfit-undo.md) · ★336)
- **paperfit-undo**: 回滚最近一次自动写回。 ([OpenRaiser/PaperFit](https://github.com/OpenRaiser/PaperFit/blob/HEAD/plugins/paperfit/commands/paperfit-undo.md) · ★336)
- **papers-index**: Build/refresh the KB's papers ledger — the one file an agent reads to understand the whole paper corpus without re-reading it. Scans Sources/Papers/*.pdf JOINed to Wiki/Summaries/*.md and records, per paper, PDF present/readable + content hash, read-depth (none/abstract/full/grounded), and verification status. Surfaces the reading backlog (readable PDFs not yet read) and the re-fetch queue (missing/unreadable PDFs). The ledger is the idempotency authority: fetch skips papers already present+readable, summarization skips papers whose PDF is unchanged. ([bobbyni819/vaultlab](https://github.com/bobbyni819/vaultlab/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/papers-index.md) · ★1)
- **papers-setup**: Set up the claude-papers MCP server globally. Installs npm dependencies and registers the server in ~/.claude.json so Papers tools are available in every project. ([knownout/claude-papers](https://github.com/knownout/claude-papers/blob/HEAD/commands/papers-setup.md))

The coordinator session only handles worktree creation, tmux orchestration, progress aggregation, and the post-completion merge train. Use when 3+ independent sub-tasks need true per-worktree skill access (Pseudo CR + Real CR + Codex Phase 7 each) without context contention. - **pause**: The founder can be tired at any point. (Tired phrasing — "sleepy", "stopping", "continue tomorrow" ([drshailesh88/build_playbook](https://github.com/drshailesh88/build_playbook/blob/HEAD/commands/pause.md))
